title: Project Knowledge Base

ms.date: 2026-11-26
ms.topic: overview

## Purpose

This folder is the project's knowledge base, a persistent second brain for the
Zava Forward research. It gathers the evidence, syntheses, working method and
project memory that agents and people need, and this index tells them where to
look first.

The design follows the LLM Wiki pattern described by Andrej Karpathy: keep
immutable sources separate from maintained syntheses, read a content index
before searching, record changes in an append-only log, and file useful
answers back into the knowledge base so they are not lost in chat history.
Topic hubs work like the Maps of Content used in personal knowledge
management: short pages that point to clusters of related material.

## How to Use This Knowledge Base

The paper's [final editorial pass](../capstone/thought-leadership/everything-but-the-software/README.md#final-editorial-pass-26-november)
shortens the article, moves audit history into the existing Supplement,
standardises the eight observed CR1 contrasts and moves the prediction-verdicts
table into Section 7. Its
[change log and numeric audit](../capstone/thought-leadership/everything-but-the-software/anc/revision-report.md#final-arxiv-release-pass-26-november)
record the before/after metrics, every numeric difference and the verified
public status of both OSF registrations alongside owner-confirmed employer
clearance and coauthor approval. Earlier revision records below are historical.

The paper's [marked author-voice revision](../capstone/thought-leadership/everything-but-the-software/README.md#marked-author-voice-revision-26-november)
starts with eight owner annotations, then applies the learned reader-context
and narrative checks across all 71 headed sections. The
[revision record](../capstone/thought-leadership/everything-but-the-software/anc/revision-report.md#marked-author-voice-revision-26-november)
tracks dispositions and validation. All 88 selected authoring tests pass;
a separate 75-page preview is source-bound and the delivered PDF is untouched.
Scientific results and original annotations are preserved.

The paper's [26 November unsupported-claim revision](../capstone/thought-leadership/everything-but-the-software/README.md#unsupported-claim-revision-26-november)
adds named-source and case-record anchors and cuts unsupported speed, cost
and causal assertions. The

records the narrower claims and evidence limits. All 88 selected authoring
checks pass; native-diagram regeneration remains unavailable. A separate
75-page preview matches the final sources. The owner-approved concurrent

demonstrated. The recommendation retains API/core/Azure SQL and defers
mandatory dedicated Fabric, while preserving a conditional read-only analytics
option for explicit portfolio/history needs. Ledger, permission/history,
private connectivity and CU/licensing are qualified. The 27 October overall
USD700 renewal through 2026-12-31 is distinct from unknown remaining headroom
and unestablished Fabric purchase permission; the source-cost comparison
includes potential loss of serverless idle-pause savings and post-trial
restoration verification. Source/contrarian research is not live validation or
adoption approval.

For #530, the [employee chat design and API guide](../../src/api/README.md#employee-chat-with-bounded-skills-530)
records one read-only chat agent, five repository-defined advisory skills,
explicit selection, role-projected grounding, minimal audit and workflow-only
fallback. Local browser/axe checks are not live employee-site, actual model,
screen-reader, voice-control or operational acceptance.

Evidence: the [whole-project Responsible AI review](./research/rai/project-rai-review.md)
assesses the 2026-10-28 source baseline across both sites, five bounded agents,
chat, briefing, IoT, data and infrastructure against NIST AI RMF 1.0. It links
the dated six-phase planner artifacts, labels evidence/hypotheses/unknowns and
proposes a backlog without creating issues. Operational approval, legal
currentness, fairness, meaningful human oversight and direct accessibility
acceptance remain unestablished; earlier RAI findings are not silently closed.

For #531, the [dated HAX design note](./research/ux/briefing-hax-design-note.md)
maps the HAX guidelines to the briefing and loading feedback, and the [design-system state inventory](./research/ux/design-system.md)
and [API loading/briefing guide](../../src/api/README.md#loading-feedback-and-todays-briefing-531)
record shared async feedback and the HAX briefing interaction: actual declared
sources and preparation time, explicit AI/rules labels, conditional draft
review and reversible local dismissal. These native Fluent-derived patterns
do not establish full Fluent adoption, staff usability, screen-reader/voice
acceptance or live employee-site cold-start verification.
The 2026-10-31 local correction executed successful draft reviews and
loading/recovery browser scenarios with zero violations in four pinned-axe
scans; three incomplete contrast checks remain human-assessment work.
The specialist note and live verification remain unmet, not issue closure.

1. Read this index first and pick the rows in
   [Task Routing](#task-routing) that match the task.
2. Read the [Project Memory](./memory.md) for owner context and preferences.
3. Open only the files the task needs. Large regulatory snapshots should be
   searched for the relevant provision, not read end to end.
4. Cite the file, section and date for every material claim, and follow the
   evidence rules in the [Regulatory Source Register](./regulatory/README.md).
5. When the work produces a durable finding, integrate it into the right
   document, update this index and add an entry to the
   [Knowledge Base Log](./log.md).

## Layers

| Layer | Location | Owner and change rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Schema | [copilot-instructions.md](../../.github/copilot-instructions.md) | Repository conventions for agents. Change only when the working method changes |
| Index | This file | Updated whenever a knowledge base file is added, moved, retired or materially changed |
| Sources | [regulatory/](./regulatory/) | Dated official snapshots. Immutable; a newer retrieval is recorded as a reviewed update |
| Syntheses | [research/](./research/) | Agent-maintained research with citations. Integrate new findings in place |
| Method | [method/](./method/) | AMA training material and reusable working prompts |
| Resources | [resources/](./resources/) | Curated pointers to internal and external resources, with retrieved findings and access notes |
| Memory | [memory.md](./memory.md) | Owner-curated notes. Agents update it only when the owner asks |
| Log | [log.md](./log.md) | Append-only record of ingests, restructures and lint passes |
| Project documents | [docs/](../) outside this folder | Problem specification and deliverables, governed by the repository instructions |

## Task Routing

For scientific-paper writing, revision or assisted review, use
[AMA Scientific Paper Writer and Reviewer](../../.github/agents/ama-scientific-paper-writer-reviewer.agent.md)
and [Scientific Paper Writing and Review](./method/scientific-paper-writing-and-review.md).
The agent separates editing from read-only review and labels generic guidance
when actual venue requirements or scientific evidence cannot be verified.
For owner-voice revisions it loads
[ama-author-voice](../../.github/skills/ama-author-voice/SKILL.md), which owns
the blog-based voice profile, marked-first revision and whole-document
reader-context/storytelling checks. Voice guidance cannot change evidence
or widen edit and publication authority.

| [Retrofit Operating-Data Exercise Pack](./research/retrofit-synthetic-data/README.md) | Version 2.0.0: fifteen months of constructed operating records for 980 candidate homes. The case presents a professional team aware of bottlenecks but not where they occur. The pack covers the tracker, stage event log, property register, CRM contacts, supplier appointments, safety holds, QA and evidence, grant and finance, capacity, board reports and issues log, with realistic record-keeping imperfections, a data dictionary and a manifest. These records are for the exercise and are not evidence of actual company performance. The answer key and generator are held outside the knowledge base so analysis can stay blind. ## Maintenance

### Ingest a New Source

1. Classify the source using the categories in the repository instructions.
2. Place an official regulatory, funding or standards snapshot in the matching
   [regulatory/](./regulatory/) topic folder and add it to the
   [Regulatory Source Register](./regulatory/README.md). Record other sources
   in the synthesis that uses them.
3. Integrate the findings into the existing synthesis or project document.
   Create a new synthesis page only when no existing page can hold the topic.
4. Flag contradictions with existing pages rather than overwriting them.
5. Update this index and append an `ingest` entry to the
   [Knowledge Base Log](./log.md).

### File Back a Durable Answer

When a question produces a comparison, analysis or finding worth keeping,
save it into the relevant synthesis or project document with its evidence,
update this index when a page is added, and log it as a `query` entry.

### Lint the Knowledge Base

Run a lint pass before a major deliverable, submission or regulatory
commencement date. Check for:

* Broken relative links and files missing from this index. Run
  `python3 tools/check-legacy-references.py` to check changed Markdown links and
  that retired `mvp/` and `prototype/` paths name archive `v0.21.0` (Refs #203)
* Contradictions between syntheses, project documents and sources
* Stale claims superseded by a newer source or a regulatory change
* Snapshots past their monitoring cadence in the register
* Orphan pages with no inbound link from this index or another page

Record the result as a `lint` entry in the log.

### Placement Rules

| Material | Location |
| --- | --- |
| Official legislation, standards, policy, guidance or funding material | `regulatory/{topic}/` |
| Sector research and benchmarks | `research/sector/` |
| Research about Zava North Homes and its roles | `research/company/` |
| AMA training material and reusable prompts | `method/` |
| Internal or external resource pointers and their retrieved findings | `resources/` |
| Owner notes and preferences | `memory.md`, only when the owner asks |
| Problem specification, BRDs, ideas and presentations | Existing folders under `docs/` |
| Private owner material, such as the cohort map | `support/`, outside the knowledge base |

## Prospective Progress Capture Routing (2026-10-17, #102)

This is the current continuation of the earlier merged-#390 route above.
Start with the [core event/writer contract](../../src/core/README.md#historical-progress-evidence-and-declared-forecasts-102),
then the [prospective programme evidence boundary](./research/ux/customer-journeys/programme.md#prospective-progress-capture-continuation-2026-10-17-102)
and [unchanged report/export API](../../src/api/README.md#historical-progress-report-and-export-102).
Existing controlled stage, handoff and per-measure quality writers now append
precise versioned events. Actual-domain JSON/SQLite/task-owned local SQL Server
evidence covers rollback, failed recovery and post-COMMIT exact-once behavior.
Past gaps, legacy precision, nonexistent general forward/closure writers and
whole-home accepted-handover actuals remain unknown. Forecast/export criteria
and the original adverse #390 Review are unchanged. quality.py overlap with
parallel #88 is declared; independent final-head acceptance remains separate.
The single original Review completed with two Medium findings. Correction ONE
retains that adverse verdict, fails closed on later contradictory handoff
states, and explicitly proves event/audit counts after SQL committed refresh
failure/retry/restart. Final-head coordinator judgment is not self-awarded.
Authorized integration of merged sensor #393 onto main943febc2 retains its
closed exports/current-generator admission and prospective event recovery.
Combined301 checks ran:274 pass,27 explicit skips; original Review/correction
ONE unchanged. Independent PR #394 final-head gate remains outstanding.
Correction TWO addresses the independent Medium failure-audit regression:
truthful sanitized operational failure/rejection after rollback, original-error
and prior-prefix retention, no false success or committed rejection.
Original adverse Review/critique/correction ONE remain unchanged; final gate
still separate from engineering evidence.
Final correction TWO integrates resident #392 on mainbcb4871b, retaining both
SQL recovery scopes and sensor boundaries. Full core942 run/847 pass/95 skips,
actual integrated MSSQL35 pass and combined/image/static checks provide
engineering evidence; PR #394 independent exact-head gate remains outstanding.
